# Agents · P · part 3

926 entries, paul-ai-researcher to perf-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **paul-ai-researcher**: Researches a chosen AI framework's official docs to produce implementation-ready guidance, best practices, syntax, core patterns, and pitfalls distilled for the specific use case. - **paul-blart**: **Charter v1.** Named for the mall cop: unglamorous, tireless, patrols the perimeter so nothing walks in unnoticed. - **paul-elder-analyst**: Use this agent when you need rigorous critical thinking analysis, evaluation of reasoning quality, systematic problem-solving, or assessment of arguments and decisions. USE PROACTIVELY when encountering complex arguments, strategic decisions, conflicting information, or situations requiring systematic intellectual rigor. Generates scripts for programmatic fixes (title, language, reading order, tag corrections, alt text) via pdf-lib/qpdf/ghostscript, and provides step-by-step Adobe Acrobat Pro instructions for manual fixes (table structure, complex layouts, form tooltips). ([Roberdan/convergio-community](https://github.com/Roberdan/convergio-community/blob/HEAD/.claude/reference/agents/pdf-accessibile.md) · ★17)
- **pdf-accessibility**: PDF document accessibility specialist. Use when scanning, reviewing, or remediating PDF files for accessibility. Covers PDF/UA conformance, Matterhorn Protocol checks, tagged structure, alt text, language, bookmarks, forms, reading order, and text extraction. Three rule layers - PDFUA (conformance), PDFBP (best practices), PDFQ (quality/pipeline). ([CIATA-BR/CIATA-DS](https://github.com/CIATA-BR/CIATA-DS/blob/HEAD/Validador/claude-code-plugin/agents/pdf-accessibility.md) · 4 copies)
- **pdf-accessibility**: &gt; Scans PDF documents for conformance with PDF/UA (ISO 14289) and the Matterhorn Protocol. ([CIATA-BR/CIATA-DS](https://github.com/CIATA-BR/CIATA-DS/blob/HEAD/Validador/docs/agents/pdf-accessibility.md) · 3 copies)

Uses check_pep7.py.\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: The user wants to check style compliance.\nuser: "Check if Objects/ follows PEP 7"\nassistant: "I'll use the pep7-style-checker to scan for PEP 7 style violations in Objects/."\n&lt;commentary&gt;\nPEP 7 compliance is important for CPython contributions and code consistency.\n&lt;/commentary&gt;\n&lt;/example&gt; ([ReviewToolkits/cpython-review-toolkit](https://github.com/ReviewToolkits/cpython-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/cpython-review-toolkit/agents/pep7-style-checker.md) · ★10)
- **pep8-reviewer**: Python style and coding-standards expert for the review-panel skill. Spawned when the diff touches .py, .pyi, or .ipynb files. - **perception-extractor**: The ONE vision pass of the analysis pipeline. LITERAL observation of ONE ad image into a standardized dataset — geometry+text (axes 1-2) AND scene+look (axes 3-4): text content + bbox + font scale + color + shadow; graphic placement + kind; canvas; medium; what-is-depicted (subjects/scene/framing) + literal light/finish facts; absence; per-axis confidence. OBSERVE-ONLY — records WHAT IS THERE / WHERE / HOW IT LOOKS, never what it MEANS. NO impression-naming (premium/honest/hero), NO register/mood, NO setting bucketing, NO real/fake judgement — those are the downstream text analysts. - **Perf Plan**: Planning & execution agent for Orleans.Lattice throughput/scaling investigations. Owns the perf issue tree (master + per-workload parent + phase children), drives the azure-throughput single-silo rig, and logs the safe throughput ceiling per workload before navigating the scaling space (WAL partitions, multi-account, multi-silo). ([NSTA1/Orleans.Lattice](https://github.com/NSTA1/Orleans.Lattice/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/perf-plan.agent.md) · ★3)
- **perf-3d**: Use after any change to src/components/three/ or src/lib/graphics.tsx. Guards the performance budget for budget Android phones on rural data. ([konankirajesh7-raj/village-verse](https://github.com/konankirajesh7-raj/village-verse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/perf-3d.md))
- **perf-accessibility**: Reviews performance and accessibility — load times, rendering, WCAG compliance, keyboard navigation ([jckeen/dotfiles](https://github.com/jckeen/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/perf-accessibility.md) · ★2)
- **perf-adversary**: Red-team performance reviewer for in-flight code diffs. Reads a git diff and produces a ranked critique focused on hot-path complexity, N+1 patterns, allocation/GC pressure, blocking I/O on async paths, and pathological scaling. One of six swarm workers spawned by hooks/adversarial-trigger.sh; safe to invoke manually. ([chriscantu/claude-config](https://github.com/chriscantu/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/perf-adversary.md))
- **perf-advisor**: stub for fixture ([elmoritz/agents-skills-setup](https://github.com/elmoritz/agents-skills-setup/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/config/valid-review-advisors/agents/perf-advisor.md) · ★7)
- **perf-agent**: Pi perf delegate for scoped implementation within an authorized handoff. Reads the poteto-mode skill before work and follows its current playbook and principle leaves. The parent owns delegation and task coordination; this agent writes only within the handoff scope. ([jiangkoumo/pi-pstack](https://github.com/jiangkoumo/pi-pstack/blob/HEAD/agents/perf-agent.md) · ★36)
